# Graph Report - universal  (2026-06-04)

## Corpus Check
- 1799 files · ~3,005,605 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 34226 nodes · 74704 edges · 1722 communities (1147 shown, 575 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 81% EXTRACTED · 19% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 14340 edges (avg confidence: 0.54)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `798e047e`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
